**Working**

$\boxed{\green{4}}38 \div 6 = 7 \boxed{\green{3}}$

Let's verify,

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 438 \div 6 \\\\
&= \dfrac{438}{6} \\\\
&= \dfrac{6 \times 73 }{6} \\\\
&= \dfrac{\cancel{6} \times 73 }{\cancel{6}} \\\\
&= 73
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the **__least__** number which can be added to $278$ to make a number which is exactly divisible by $13?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the **__least__** number which can be added to $278$ to make a number which is exactly divisible by $13?$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If we divide $278$ by $13$, the quotient is $21$ and the remainder is $5$.

So we can add $13 - 5 = 8$ to $278$ to make it exactly divisible by $13$.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$8$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If we divide $278$ by $13$, the quotient is $21$ and the remainder is $5$.
<emptyline>
So we can add $13 - 5 = 8$ to $278$ to make it exactly divisible by $13$.
</code></pre>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

If the rules about what order to do arithmetic operations do **not** apply for this last question on the paper then $2 + 4 \times 3$ could be $2 + 12 = 14$ or $6 \times 3 = 18$.

Of the **five different** answers that there could be for the following question

$14 - 3 \times 4 - 2 =$

depending on the order you use for the operations, which answer would be in the middle if the answers were placed in descending order (from largest to smallest)?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If the rules about what order to do arithmetic operations do **not** apply for this last question on the paper then $2 + 4 \times 3$ could be $2 + 12 = 14$ or $6 \times 3 = 18$.
<emptyline>
Of the **five different** answers that there could be for the following question
<emptyline>
$14 - 3 \times 4 - 2 =$
<emptyline>
depending on the order you use for the operations, which answer would be in the middle if the answers were placed in descending order (from largest to smallest)?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let's use brackets to show the sequence in which we apply the operations and give precedence to operators.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Solution } 1  &= 14 - 3 \times 4 - 2 \\\\
                    &= (14 - 3) \times (4 - 2) \\\\
                    &= 11 \times 2 \\\\
                    &= 22
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Solution } 2  &= 14 - 3 \times 4 - 2 \\\\
                    &= (14 - 3) \times  4 - 2 \\\\
                    &= 11 \times 4 - 2 \\\\
                    &= (11 \times 4) - 2 \\\\
                    &= 44 - 2 \\\\
                    &= 42
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Solution } 3  &= 14 - 3 \times 4 - 2 \\\\
                    &= 14 - (3 \times 4) - 2 \\\\                   
                    &= 14 - 12 - 2 \\\\
                    &= (14 - 12) - 2 \\\\
                    &= 2 - 2 \\\\
                    &= 0
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Solution } 4  &= 14 - 3 \times 4 - 2 \\\\
                    &= 14 - (3 \times 4) - 2 \\\\
                    &= 14 - 12 - 2 \\\\
                    &= 14 - (12 - 2) \\\\
                    &= 14 - 10 \\\\
                    &= 4
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Solution } 5  &= 14 - 3 \times 4 - 2 \\\\
                    &= 14 - 3 \times (4 - 2) \\\\
                    &= 14 - 3 \times 2\\\\
                    &= 14 - (3 \times 2) \\\\
                    &= 14 - 6 \\\\
                    &= 8
\end{aligned}
$

Let's put them in descending order

$42, \quad 22, \quad 8, \quad 4, \quad 0$

Answer in the middle $= 8$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$8$

</div>
</div>

\end{aligned}

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1221 \div 11 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1221} {11} \\\\
&= \dfrac {11 \times 111} {11} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{11} \times 111} {\cancel{11}} \\\\
&= 111
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Complete all the entries in the table below showing the sum, difference, product and quotient for pairs of numbers from the first two columns. The first row is completed as an example to help you.


{/*
**$1 ^\text{st}$ number**  ,**$2 ^\text{nd}$ number**     ,**SUM**    ,**DIFFERENCE**     ,**PRODUCT**    ,**QUOTIENT**
$6$             ,$2$                ,$8$        ,$4$                ,$12$           ,$3$
$12$            ,$3$                ,$\ldots$   ,$\ldots$           ,$\ldots$       ,$\ldots$
$\ldots$        ,$\ldots$           ,$15$       ,$5$                ,$\ldots$       ,$\ldots$
$\ldots$        ,$\ldots$           ,$\ldots$   ,$2$                ,$8$            ,$\ldots$
$\ldots$        ,$\ldots$           ,$\ldots$   ,$\ldots$           ,$18$           ,$2$
$\ldots$        ,$\ldots$           ,$21$       ,$\ldots$           ,$\ldots$       ,$6$
*/}

| **$1 ^\text{st}$ number**     | **$2 ^\text{nd}$ number**     |  **SUM**     | **DIFFERENCE**     | **PRODUCT**     | **QUOTIENT**     |
|:--------------:    |:--------------:    |:--------:    |:--------------:    |:-----------:    |:------------:    |
|       $6$          |       $2$          |    $8$       |       $4$          |     $12$        |      $3$         |
|      $12$          |       $3$          | $\ldots$     |    $\ldots$        |   $\ldots$      |   $\ldots$       |
|    $\ldots$        |    $\ldots$        |   $15$       |       $5$          |   $\ldots$      |   $\ldots$       |
|    $\ldots$        |    $\ldots$        | $\ldots$     |       $2$          |     $8$         |   $\ldots$       |
|    $\ldots$        |    $\ldots$        | $\ldots$     |    $\ldots$        |     $18$        |      $2$         |
|    $\ldots$        |    $\ldots$        |   $21$       |    $\ldots$        |   $\ldots$      |      $6$         |

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Complete all the entries in the table below showing the sum, difference, product and quotient for pairs of numbers from the first two columns. The first row is completed as an example to help you.
<emptyline>

{/*
**$1 ^\text{st}$ number**  ,**$2 ^\text{nd}$ number**     ,**SUM**    ,**DIFFERENCE**     ,**PRODUCT**    ,**QUOTIENT**

$12$            ,$3$                ,$\ldots$   ,$\ldots$           ,$\ldots$       ,$\ldots$
$\ldots$        ,$\ldots$           ,$15$       ,$5$                ,$\ldots$       ,$\ldots$
$\ldots$        ,$\ldots$           ,$\ldots$   ,$2$                ,$8$            ,$\ldots$
$\ldots$        ,$\ldots$           ,$\ldots$   ,$\ldots$           ,$18$           ,$2$
$\ldots$        ,$\ldots$           ,$21$       ,$\ldots$           ,$\ldots$       ,$6$
*/}
<emptyline>
| **$1 ^\text{st}$ number**     | **$2 ^\text{nd}$ number**     |  **SUM**     | **DIFFERENCE**     | **PRODUCT**     | **QUOTIENT**     |
|:--------------:    |:--------------:    |:--------:    |:--------------:    |:-----------:    |:------------:    |
|       $6$          |       $2$          |    $8$       |       $4$          |     $12$        |      $3$         |
|      $12$          |       $3$          | $\ldots$     |    $\ldots$        |   $\ldots$      |   $\ldots$       |
|    $\ldots$        |    $\ldots$        |   $15$       |       $5$          |   $\ldots$      |   $\ldots$       |
|    $\ldots$        |    $\ldots$        | $\ldots$     |       $2$          |     $8$         |   $\ldots$       |
|    $\ldots$        |    $\ldots$        | $\ldots$     |    $\ldots$        |     $18$        |      $2$         |
|    $\ldots$        |    $\ldots$        |   $21$       |    $\ldots$        |   $\ldots$      |      $6$         |
</code></pre>

<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

**ROW 2**

$1$st number and $2$nd number are known so rest can be solved.

**ROW 3**

Sum $= 154$

Difference $= 5$

$
\begin{aligned}
             15 &= x + y \\
             5  &= x - y  \\
         15 + 5 &= x + y + (x - y) \\
             20 &= x + y + x -y \\
                &= x + x + y -y \\
                &= 2x \\
\dfrac {20}{10} &= x \\
              x &= 10 \\
              y &= 10 - 5 = 5 \\
\end{aligned}
$

**ROW 4**

Product $8$ 

$8$ is only a multiple of $4 \times 2$

So the numbers are $2$ and $4$

**ROW 5**

Product is $18$

$18$ is a multiple of :

$9 \times 2$ but $9$ is not divisible by $2$ to give quotient $2$

$6 \times 3$ are the numbers with quotient $2$

**ROW 6**

Sum $= 21$

Quotient $= 6$

Let's find multiples of $6$ $<21$ and $>6$  

$6 \times 2 = 12$

$6 + 2 = 12 \not 21$

$6 \times 3 = 18$

$18 \div 3 = 6$

$18 + 3 = 21$

The numbers are $18$ and $3$
</div>

**Working**

| **$1 ^\text{st}$ number**     | **$2 ^\text{nd}$ number**     |  **SUM**     | **DIFFERENCE**     | **PRODUCT**     | **QUOTIENT**     |
|:------------------:|:------------------:|:------------------:|:----------------:|:--------------------------:|:--------------------------:|
|      $12$          |       $3$          |    $12 + 3=15$     |    $12 - 3=9$    |    $12 \times 3 = 36$      |     $12 \div  4 = 3$       |
|      $10$          |       $5$          |    $10 + 5=15$     |    $10 - 5=5$    |    $10 \times 5 = 50$      |     $10 \div  5 = 2$       |
|      $10$          |       $5$          |    $4 + 2 = 6$     |     $4 - 2=2$    |      $4 \times 2 = 8$      |      $4 \div  2 = 2$       |
|       $6$          |       $3$          |    $6 + 3 = 9$     |     $6 - 3=3$    |     $6 \times 3 = 18$      |      $6 \div  3 = 2$       |
|      $18$          |       $3$          |   $18 + 3 = 21$    |   $18 - 3=15$    |    $18 \times 3 = 54$      |     $18 \div  3 = 6$       |
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Which of the following fractions is closet to $\dfrac 1 4$

$\dfrac {11} {40} \quad,\dfrac {16} {60} \quad ,\dfrac {21} {80} \quad ,\dfrac {26} {100} \quad,\dfrac {31} {120}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Which of the following fractions is closet to $\dfrac 1 4$
<emptyline>
$\dfrac {11} {40} \quad,\dfrac {16} {60} \quad ,\dfrac {21} {80} \quad ,\dfrac {26} {100} \quad,\dfrac {31} {120}$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

To determine which fraction is closest to $\dfrac {1}{4}$, we can subtract $\dfrac {1}{4}$ from each fraction. The difference that is closest to zero will indicate the fraction that is closest to $\dfrac {1}{4}$.

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {11} {40},  \quad \dfrac {4} {15},  \quad   \dfrac {21} {80},  \quad   \dfrac {13} {50}, \quad   \dfrac {31} {120} \\\\
&= \bigg (\dfrac {11} {40} - \dfrac {1} {4}\bigg ),  \quad \bigg (\dfrac {4} {15}- \dfrac {1} {4}\bigg ),  \quad   \bigg (\dfrac {21} {80} - \dfrac {1} {4}\bigg ),  \quad   \bigg (\dfrac {13} {50} - \dfrac {1} {4}\bigg ), \quad   \bigg (\dfrac {31} {120}- \dfrac {1} {4}\bigg )\\\\
&= \bigg (\dfrac {11} {40} - \dfrac {1 \times 10} {4 \times 10}\bigg ),  \quad \bigg (\dfrac {4\times 4} {15\times 4}- \dfrac {1\times 15} {4\times 15}\bigg ),  \quad   \bigg (\dfrac {21} {80} - \dfrac {1\times 20} {4 \times 20}\bigg ),  \quad   \bigg (\dfrac {13 \times 2} {50\times 2} - \dfrac {1 \times 25} {4\times 25}\bigg ), \quad   \bigg (\dfrac {31} {120}- \dfrac {1 \times 30} {4\times 30}\bigg )\\\\
&= \bigg (\dfrac {11 - 10} {40} \bigg ),  \quad \bigg (\dfrac {16-15} {60}\bigg ),  \quad   \bigg (\dfrac {21 -20} {80}\bigg ),  \quad   \bigg (\dfrac {26- 25} {100} \bigg ), \quad   \bigg (\dfrac {31 - 30} {120}\bigg )\\\\
&= \dfrac{1}{40}, \quad \dfrac{1}{60}, \quad \dfrac{1}{80}, \quad   \dfrac{1}{100}, \quad   \dfrac {1} {120}
\end{aligned}
$

The fraction with the biggest denominator is the smallest fraction and is nearer to zero.

The fraction nearest to $\dfrac {1}{4}$ is $= \dfrac {31}{120}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac {31} {120}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

<emptyline>

&= \bigg (\dfrac {11} {40} - \dfrac {1} {4}\bigg ),  \quad \bigg (\dfrac {4} {15}- \dfrac {1} {4}\bigg ),  \quad   \bigg (\dfrac {21} {80} - \dfrac {1} {4}\bigg ),  \quad   \bigg (\dfrac {13} {50} - \dfrac {1} {4}\bigg ), \quad   \bigg (\dfrac {31} {120}- \dfrac {1} {4}\bigg )\\\\
&= \bigg (\dfrac {11} {40} - \dfrac {1 \times 10} {4 \times 10}\bigg ),  \quad \bigg (\dfrac {4\times 4} {15\times 4}- \dfrac {1\times 15} {4\times 15}\bigg ),  \quad   \bigg (\dfrac {21} {80} - \dfrac {1\times 20} {4 \times 20}\bigg ),  \quad   \bigg (\dfrac {13 \times 2} {50\times 2} - \dfrac {1 \times 25} {4\times 25}\bigg ), \quad   \bigg (\dfrac {31} {120}- \dfrac {1 \times 30} {4\times 30}\bigg )\\\\
&= \bigg (\dfrac {11 - 10} {40} \bigg ),  \quad \bigg (\dfrac {16-15} {60}\bigg ),  \quad   \bigg (\dfrac {21 -20} {80}\bigg ),  \quad   \bigg (\dfrac {26- 25} {100} \bigg ), \quad   \bigg (\dfrac {31 - 30} {120}\bigg )\\\\

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Make a list of all the whole numbers which leave a remainder of $7$ when divided into $46$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Make a list of all the whole numbers which leave a remainder of $7$ when divided into $46$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

The whole number leaves a remainder $7$ when divided into $46$.

As such, the number will be a factor of $= 46 - 7 = 39$

Factors of $39 = 1, 3 , 13$ and $39$.

The number has to be greater than $7$ as it leaves a remainder $7$.

Lets check $13$ and $39$ fulfil the criteria.

$= \dfrac {46} {13} =$ Quotient $3$ Remainder $7$

$= \dfrac {46} {39} =$ Quotient $1$ Remainder $7$

The whole numbers which leave a remainder of $7$ when dividied into $46 = 13$ and $39$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$13, 39$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Fill in the spaces in the following table. Leave all fractions in their lowest form.

The first line has been done for you.

|     Fraction         |      Decimal        |     Percentage     | 
|:-------------------: |:------------------: |:-----------------: |
| $\dfrac {1} {2}$     |  $0.5$              |   $50\%$           |
| $\dfrac {1} {4}$     |                     |                    |
|                      |                     |   $10\%$           |
|                      |  $0.85$             |                    |


</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Fill in the spaces in the following table. Leave all fractions in their lowest form.
<emptyline>
The first line has been done for you.
<emptyline>
|     Fraction         |      Decimal        |     Percentage     | 
|:-------------------: |:------------------: |:-----------------: |
| $\dfrac {1} {2}$     |  $0.5$              |   $50\%$           |
| $\dfrac {1} {4}$     |                     |                    |
|                      |                     |   $10\%$           |
|                      |  $0.85$             |                    |
<emptyline>
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

**ROW 1**

Given that,

Fraction $= \dfrac{1}{4}$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Decimal} &= \dfrac{1}{4} \\\\
&= \dfrac{1}{4} \times \dfrac{25}{25} \\\\
&= \dfrac{25}{100} \\\\
&= 0.25
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Percentage} &= \dfrac{1}{4} \times 100 \% \\\\
&= \dfrac{100}{4} \% \\\\
&= 25\%
\end{aligned}
$

**ROW 2**

Given that,

Percentage = $10\%$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Fraction} &= \dfrac{10}{100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{1 \cancel 0}{10 \cancel 0} \\\\
&= \dfrac{1}{10}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Decimal} &= \dfrac{10}{100} \\\\
&= 0.1
\end{aligned}
$

**ROW 3**

Given that,

Decimal = $0.85$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Fraction} &= 0.85 \times \dfrac{100}{100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{85}{100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{17}{20}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Percentage} &= 0.85 \times 100 \% \\\\
&= 85 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the smallest whole number, above $120$, which when divided by $53$, leaves a remainder of two.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the smallest whole number, above $120$, which when divided by $53$, leaves a remainder of two.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$53 \times 2 = 106$ ,
$106 < 120$

$53 \times 3 = 159$ ,
$159 > 120$ 

$159 + 2 = 161$

The smallest whole number above $120$ which when divided by $53$ leaves a remainder of two $= 161$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$161$

</div>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$53 \times 2 = 106$ ,
$106 < 120$
<emptyline>

<emptyline>

</code></pre>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Circle the number nearest to $0.1$

$0.2\quad 0.101\quad 0.11\quad 0.99\quad 0.0998\quad 1.0$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Circle the number nearest to $0.1$
<emptyline>
$0.2\quad 0.101\quad 0.11\quad 0.99\quad 0.0998\quad 1.0$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

To compare, we can convert all the numbers to fractions, decimals or percentages.

Let's put them in ascending order now.

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 0.2, \quad 0.101, \quad 0.11, \quad 0.99, \quad 0.0998, \quad 1.0 \\\\
&= 0.0998, \quad 0.99, \quad 0.101, \quad 0.11, \quad 0.2, \quad 1.0
\end{aligned}
$

$0.1$ lies between $0.101$ and $0.11$

The number nearest to $0.1$ = $0.101$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$0.101$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Bob is thinking of a number. Alan asks him some questions.

Is it more than $40?$ Yes

Is it a multiple of $3?$ Yes

Is it a multiple of $5?$ Yes

Is it more than $60?$ No


What numbers could Bob be thinking of?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Bob is thinking of a number. Alan asks him some questions.
<emptyline>
Is it more than $40?$ Yes
<emptyline>
Is it a multiple of $3?$ Yes
<emptyline>
Is it a multiple of $5?$ Yes
<emptyline>
Is it more than $60?$ No
<emptyline>

What numbers could Bob be thinking of?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the number Bob thinks = $a$

As per the question,

$a > 40$

$a$ is a multiple of $3$

$a$ is a multiple of $5$

$a <60$

Numbers between $40$ and $60$ which are multiple of $5 = {45, 50, 55}$

Numbers between $40$ and $60$ which are multiple of $3 = {45, 48, 51, 54, 57}$

Since $45$ is common in both, 

$a = 45$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$45$

</div>
</div>

<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

&= 10 + 2 \\\\
&= 12 \ \cancel {=} \ 6
\end{aligned}
$

$30 \div 3 + 2 = 6$        $\boxed {\times}$

<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

\begin{aligned}

&= \dfrac {24} {4} - 3 \times 2 \\\\
&= \dfrac {6 \times 4} {4} - 3 \times 2 \\\\
&= \dfrac {12 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} - 3 \times 2 \\\\
&= 6 - 6 \\\\
&= 0
\end{aligned}
$

$24 \div 4 - 3 \times 2 = 0$        $\boxed {\times}$

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
&= 24 \div 4 - 3 \times 2 \\\\
&= \dfrac {24} {4} - 3 \times 2 \\\\
&= \dfrac {6 \times 4} {4} - 3 \times 2 \\\\
&= \dfrac {12 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} - 3 \times 2 \\\\
&= 6 - 6 \\\\
&= 0
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
$24 \div 4 - 3 \times 2 = 0$        $\boxed {\times}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

**Question**

$4.68 \div 6$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$4.68 \div 6$
</code></pre>

<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let's first divide the numbers ignoring the decimal points.

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 4.68 \div 6 \\\\
&= \dfrac {468} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {6 \times 78} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {6} \times 78} {\cancel{6}} \\\\
&= 78
\end{aligned}
$

Between the two numbers, the total number of digits after the decimal point is two.

Let's account for the decimal point now.

$= 0.78$
</div>
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A cup contains $250 \ \text{millimetres}$. Roughly how many litres is this?

Put a ring round the best answer.
 
- $\text{One-quarter}$  
- $2.5$  
- $25$


</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A cup contains $250 \ \text{millimetres}$. Roughly how many litres is this?
<emptyline>
Put a ring round the best answer.
<emptyline>
- $\text{One-quarter}$  
- $2.5$  
- $25$
<emptyline>
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
1 \ \text {millilitre}  &= \dfrac {1} {1000} \ \text {litres} \\\\
250 \times 1 \ \text {millilitre}  &= 250 \times \dfrac {1} {1000} \ \text {litres} \\\\
250 \ \text {millilitres}          &= \dfrac {250} {1000} \ \text {litres} \\\\
250 \ \text {millilitres}          &= \dfrac {250} {250 \times 4} \ \text {litres} \\\\
250 \ \text {millilitres}          &= \dfrac {\cancel {250}} {\cancel {250} \times 4} \ \text {litres} \\\\
250 \ \text {millilitres}          &= \dfrac {1} {4} \ \text {litres} \\\\
250 \ \text {millilitres}          &= 0.250 \ \text {litres}
\end{aligned}
$

$0.250 \ \text {litres}$ is One quarter of a litre.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\text {One-quarter}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
1 \ \text {millilitre}  &= \dfrac {1} {1000} \ \text {litres} \\\\
250 \times 1 \ \text {millilitre}  &= 250 \times \dfrac {1} {1000} \ \text {litres} \\\\
250 \ \text {millilitres}          &= \dfrac {250} {1000} \ \text {litres} \\\\
250 \ \text {millilitres}          &= \dfrac {250} {250 \times 4} \ \text {litres} \\\\
250 \ \text {millilitres}          &= \dfrac {\cancel {250}} {\cancel {250} \times 4} \ \text {litres} \\\\
250 \ \text {millilitres}          &= \dfrac {1} {4} \ \text {litres} \\\\
250 \ \text {millilitres}          &= 0.250 \ \text {litres}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
$0.250 \ \text {litres}$ is One quarter of a litre.
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$\text {One-quarter}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The diagram shows part of a number line.

What number is the arrow pointing to? Write your answer in the box.

![papers/city-of-london-school/2008/sample-paper-group-2/s01-q10-s01-i00.png](/assets/papers/city-of-london-school/2008/sample-paper-group-2/s01-q10-s01-i00.png "papers/city-of-london-school/2008/sample-paper-group-2/s01-q10-s01-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The diagram shows part of a number line.
<emptyline>
What number is the arrow pointing to? Write your answer in the box.
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A palindromic number is the same whether read from left to right or right to left. For example $41514$ is a palindromic number.

Find as many whole numbers as you can which are:

- greater than $1$ and less than $500$.
- palindromic
- either a square number or a cube number.

Do your working below the answer line.

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Using each of the digits $2, 4$ and $9,$ find two three-digit numbers which have both $2$ and $7$ as factors.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is half of $1.01?$

- $0.55$
- $0.505$
- $0.5005$
- $0.055$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is half of $1.01?$
<emptyline>
- $0.55$
- $0.505$
- $0.5005$
- $0.055$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \text {half of} \ 1.01 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1.01} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {0.505 \times 2} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {0.505 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
&= 0.505
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is $2010 + (+2010) + (-2010) - (+ 2010) - (-2010)?$

- $0$
- $2010$
- $4020$
- $6030$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is $2010 + (+2010) + (-2010) - (+ 2010) - (-2010)?$
<emptyline>
- $0$
- $2010$
- $4020$
- $6030$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 2010 + (+2010) + (-2010) - (+ 2010) - (-2010) \\\\
&= 2010 + 2010 - 2010 - 2010 + 2010 \\\\
&= 2010 - 2010 + 2010 -2010 + 2010 \\\\
&= 0 + 0 + 2010 \\\\
&= 2010
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

$(14.7 + 5.3) \div 4 = $

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$(14.7 + 5.3) \div 4 = $
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= (14.7 + 5.3) \div 4 \\\\
&= 20 \div 4 \\\\
&= \dfrac {20} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times 5} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {4} \times 5} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
&= 5
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Show your working out.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 47 \% \ \text {of} \ 90 \\\\
&= (40 \% + 5 \% + 2 \%) \ \text {of} \ 90 \\\\
&= 40 \% \ \text {of} \ 90 + 5 \% \ \text {of} \ 90 + 2 \% \ \text {of} \ 90 \\\\
&= 4 \times 10 \% \ \text {of} \ 90 + 4.5 + 2 \times 1 \% \ \text {of} \ 90 \\\\
&= 4 \times 9 + 4.5 + 2 \times 0.9 \\\\
&= 36 + 4.5 + 1.8 \\\\
&= 42.3
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Katie had a pack of twenty cards numbered from $1$ to $20$.

$\large {\boxed{1} \quad \boxed{2} \quad \boxed{3} \quad \boxed{4} \quad \boxed{5} \quad \boxed{6} \quad \boxed{7} \quad \boxed{8} \quad \boxed{9} \quad \boxed{10} }$

$\large {\boxed{11} \quad \boxed{12} \quad \boxed{13} \quad \boxed{14} \quad \boxed{15} \quad \boxed{16} \quad \boxed{17} \quad \boxed{18} \quad \boxed{19} \quad \boxed{20} }$

She arranged the cards into six unequal piles.

The numbers on the cards in each pile added to the same total. 

What was the total and how could this be done?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<emptyline>
$\large {\boxed{11} \quad \boxed{12} \quad \boxed{13} \quad \boxed{14} \quad \boxed{15} \quad \boxed{16} \quad \boxed{17} \quad \boxed{18} \quad \boxed{19} \quad \boxed{20} }$
<emptyline>
She arranged the cards into six unequal piles.
<emptyline>
The numbers on the cards in each pile added to the same total. 
<emptyline>
What was the total and how could this be done?
</code></pre>

<div class="papername">
<p>latymer-upper-school--11-plus--maths--2014--sample-paper-1:1:17</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>Waffles Woof</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>rag_wf_notstarted</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">

tnumber

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I have lots of $5\ \text{p}$, $8\ \text{p}$ and $11\ \text{p}$ stamps. What's the biggest amount I cannot make exactly with my stamps?

- $7\ \text{p}$
- $49\ \text{p}$
- $19\ \text{p}$
- $38\ \text{p}$
- $17\ \text{p}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I have lots of $5\ \text{p}$, $8\ \text{p}$ and $11\ \text{p}$ stamps. What's the biggest amount I cannot make exactly with my stamps?
<emptyline>
- $7\ \text{p}$
- $49\ \text{p}$
- $19\ \text{p}$
- $38\ \text{p}$
- $17\ \text{p}$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$17\ \text{p}$

Lets verify

$
\begin{aligned}
7\ \text{p}  &= 5 \ \text{p} \times 2 + 8\ \text{p} -11\ \text{p}\\\\
49\ \text{p}   &= 11 \ \text{p} \times 4 + 5\ \text{p} \\\\
19\ \text{p}   &= 11 \ \text{p} + 8 \ \text{p} \\\\
38\ \text{p}   &= 11 \ \text{p} \times 3 + 5 \ \text{p} 
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>E</p>
</div>

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$17\ \text{p}$

<emptyline>

\begin{aligned}
7\ \text{p}  &= 5 \ \text{p} \times 2 + 8\ \text{p} -11\ \text{p}\\\\
49\ \text{p}   &= 11 \ \text{p} \times 4 + 5\ \text{p} \\\\
19\ \text{p}   &= 11 \ \text{p} + 8 \ \text{p} \\\\
38\ \text{p}   &= 11 \ \text{p} \times 3 + 5 \ \text{p} 
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
